<script>
    $().ready(function () {
        const meID = '#ctlBodyPane_ctl00_ctl01_dynamicSummary_divSummary';

        $(meID).find('.btnCollapsibleList').on('click', function (event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.stopPropagation();

            const elementID = $(event.target).parent().find('.divCollapsibleList').attr('id');
            const buttonID = $(event.target).parent().find('.btnCollapsibleList').attr('id');

            Beacon.WCAG.ExpandCollapse([elementID], null, buttonID);
        });
    });
</script>

<script>
    function isNumber(evt, obj, numLen) {
        evt = (evt) ? evt : window.event;
        var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : evt.keyCode;

        if ((charCode == 46 && obj.value.indexOf('.') > 0) ||
            (charCode != 46 && charCode > 31 && (charCode < 48 || charCode > 57)))
            return false;

        // Throw out anything that will create an overflow...AL
        if (obj.value >= 99999999)
            return false;

        // If specififed, throw out the number lenghts that exeed the defined amount...AL
        if (numLen && obj.value.toString().length > numLen - 1)
            return false;

        return true;
    }
</script>
</div>

    $(function () {
        var i = $('.DelayedMapLoad');

        i.on('load', function () {            
            if ($(this).width() == 1) {
                $("header[moduleid='31379']").parent('section').hide();

            }
